| ![\[Uncaptioned image\]](2509.07996v4/figures/icons/metrics.png) Generation Quality - Perceptual Fidelity |  |  |  |  |
|  FID | $`\downarrow`$ |  Fréchet Inception Distance |  Statistical distance between multivariate Gaussians fitted to Inception features of real and generated samples, measuring distributional similarity. | \[[99](#bib.bib99)\] |
|  FVD | $`\downarrow`$ |  Fréchet Video Distance |  Statistical distance between multivariate Gaussians fitted to I3D features \[[34](#bib.bib34)\] of real and generated videos, capturing temporal coherence. | \[[284](#bib.bib284)\] |
|  FRD | $`\downarrow`$ |  Fréchet Range Distance |  Statistical distance between Gaussians fitted to RangeNet++ features \[[217](#bib.bib217)\] extracted from LiDAR range images, assessing distributional fidelity. | \[[225](#bib.bib225)\] |
|  FPD | $`\downarrow`$ |  Fréchet Point Cloud Distance |  Statistical distance between Gaussians fitted to PointNet features \[[246](#bib.bib246)\] of raw 3D point clouds, evaluating geometric realism. | \[[266](#bib.bib266)\] |
|  FSVD | $`\downarrow`$ |  Fréchet Sparse Volume Distance |  Statistical distance between Gaussians fitted to volumetric encoder features of sparse voxel inputs, capturing volumetric structure. | \[[250](#bib.bib250)\] |
|  FPVD | $`\downarrow`$ |  Fréchet Point Volume Distance |  Statistical distance between Gaussians fitted to volumetric encoder features of hybrid point–voxel representations, measuring fidelity. | \[[250](#bib.bib250)\] |
|  F3D | $`\downarrow`$ |  Fréchet 3D Distance |  Statistical distance between Gaussians fitted to occupancy grid features, evaluating volumetric realism in generated 3D data. | \[[195](#bib.bib195)\] |
|  S-FRD | $`\downarrow`$ |  Semantic Fréchet Range Distance |  Class-aware extension of FRD that incorporates semantic labels for improved alignment of LiDAR range distributions. | \[[413](#bib.bib413)\] |
|  S-FPD | $`\downarrow`$ |  Semantic Fréchet Point Distance |  Class-aware extension of FPD that integrates semantic labels to assess alignment of 3D point cloud distributions. | \[[413](#bib.bib413)\] |
|  KID | $`\downarrow`$ |  Kernel Inception Distance |  Maximum Mean Discrepancy between Inception features using a polynomial kernel, providing an unbiased distributional similarity measure. | \[[22](#bib.bib22)\] |
|  IS | $`\uparrow`$ |  Inception Score |  Evaluates image realism by rewarding confident and diverse class predictions from a pretrained Inception classifier, without real reference data. | \[[257](#bib.bib257)\] |
|  IQ | $`\uparrow`$ |  Image Quality |  Predicts perceptual image quality by estimating human opinion scores with a learned quality assessor \[[140](#bib.bib140)\], without ground truth references. | \[[131](#bib.bib131)\] |
| ![\[Uncaptioned image\]](2509.07996v4/figures/icons/metrics.png) Generation Quality - Statistical Fidelity |  |  |  |  |
|  PR | $`\uparrow`$ |  Precision-Recall |  Reports sample fidelity as precision and distributional coverage as recall, characterizing closeness to the real data manifold. | \[[20](#bib.bib20)\] |
|  SWD | $`\downarrow`$ |  Sliced Wasserstein Distance |  Mean Wasserstein distance over multiple random 1D projections of image patches at different scales, reflecting distributional similarity. | \[[95](#bib.bib95)\] |
|  JSD | $`\downarrow`$ |  Jensen–Shannon Divergence |  Symmetric divergence measuring similarity between occupancy histograms of real and generated scenes, lower indicating better alignment. | \[[421](#bib.bib421)\] |
|  MMD | $`\downarrow`$ |  Minimum Matching Distance |  Average Chamfer distance from each real sample to its nearest generated neighbor, quantifying geometric fidelity. | \[[421](#bib.bib421)\] |
|  COV | $`\uparrow`$ |  Coverage |  Fraction of real samples matched by at least one generated output, measuring generative diversity and recall. | \[[146](#bib.bib146)\] |
|  1-NNA | \- |  1-Nearest-Neighbor Accuracy |  Overlap test using a 1-NN classifier trained across sets, where accuracy near 50% indicates distributional equivalence. | \[[146](#bib.bib146)\] |
|  Diversity | $`\uparrow`$ |  - |  Degree of variability across generated outputs for fixed prompts, often measured via pixel- or feature-wise variance. | \[[411](#bib.bib411)\] |
| ![\[Uncaptioned image\]](2509.07996v4/figures/icons/metrics.png) Generation Quality - Spatial Consistency |  |  |  |  |
|  VCS | $`\uparrow`$ |  View Consistency Score |  Summation of LoFTR keypoint confidences \[[270](#bib.bib270)\] across overlapping views, evaluating multi-view geometric consistency and alignment quality. | \[[273](#bib.bib273)\] |
|  KPM | $`\uparrow`$ |  Key Points Matching |  Ratio of successfully matched keypoints between adjacent generated and real views, reflecting geometric alignment quality. | \[[304](#bib.bib304)\] |
|  DAS | $`\downarrow`$ |  Depth Alignment Score |  Statistical discrepancy between projected point clouds and estimated monocular depth \[[354](#bib.bib354)\], measuring scene-level depth consistency. | \[[332](#bib.bib332)\] |
| ![\[Uncaptioned image\]](2509.07996v4/figures/icons/metrics.png) Generation Quality - Temporal Consistency |  |  |  |  |
|  CTC | $`\uparrow`$ |  CLIP Temporal Consistency |  Cosine similarity of CLIP features \[[249](#bib.bib249)\] across consecutive frames, measuring temporal stability and smoothness in generated video sequences. | \[[131](#bib.bib131)\] |
|  DTC | $`\uparrow`$ |  DINO Temporal Consistency |  Cosine similarity of DINO features \[[235](#bib.bib235)\] across adjacent frames, evaluating temporal coherence and smooth transitions in generated sequences. | \[[131](#bib.bib131)\] |
|  TTCE | $`\downarrow`$ |  Temporal Transformation Consistency Error |  Registration error between temporally generated and ground-truth point clouds, evaluating motion alignment and temporal consistency. | \[[173](#bib.bib173)\] |
|  CTC | $`\downarrow`$ |  Chamfer Temporal Consistency |  Chamfer distance between generated point clouds across different timestamps, quantifying temporal stability and geometric coherence. | \[[173](#bib.bib173)\] |
|  ICP | $`\downarrow`$ |  ICP Energy / Outlier |  Registration residuals and outlier ratios from Iterative Closest Point alignment of LiDAR frames, detecting temporal jitter and misalignment. | \[[422](#bib.bib422)\] |
| ![\[Uncaptioned image\]](2509.07996v4/figures/icons/metrics.png) Generation Quality - Subject Consistency |  |  |  |  |
|  SC | $`\uparrow`$ |  Subject Consistency |  Cosine similarity of subject-region features \[[235](#bib.bib235)\] across frames, evaluating identity persistence and stability in generated video sequences. | \[[121](#bib.bib121)\] |
|  FDC | $`\uparrow`$ |  Foreground Detection Confidence |  Confidence scores of detected foreground objects in generated samples using a pretrained detector, reflecting semantic plausibility and realism. | \[[173](#bib.bib173)\] |
|  CFCA | $`\uparrow`$ |  Conditional Foreground Classification Accuracy |  Semantic consistency of generated objects evaluated by classification accuracy with a pretrained object classifier, conditioned on ground truth. | \[[173](#bib.bib173)\] |
|  CFSC | $`\uparrow`$ |  Conditional Foreground Spatial Consistency |  Mean IoU between 3D boxes regressed by a conditional VAE and ground truth boxes, assessing geometric alignment under conditioning. | \[[173](#bib.bib173)\] |
| ![\[Uncaptioned image\]](2509.07996v4/figures/icons/metrics.png) Generation Quality - Controllability |  |  |  |  |
|  CDA | $`\uparrow`$ |  Conditional Detection Accuracy |  Standard detection accuracy from a pretrained 3D detector applied to generated point clouds with box conditioning, measuring semantic fidelity. | \[[173](#bib.bib173)\] |
|  CLIP-Sim | $`\uparrow`$ |  CLIP Similarity |  Average cosine similarity between CLIP embeddings of generated and reference frames, reflecting semantic alignment across modalities. | \[[205](#bib.bib205)\] |
|  MAE | $`\downarrow`$ |  Mean Absolute Error |  Difference in predicted versus reference object counts within scene graphs, assessing accuracy of graph-level controllability. | \[[196](#bib.bib196)\] |
|  JI | $`\uparrow`$ |  Jaccard Index |  Overlap ratio of predicted and reference category sets within scene graphs, evaluating graph-level semantic consistency. | \[[196](#bib.bib196)\] |
|  RotErr | $`\downarrow`$ |  Rotation Error |  Angular difference between recovered and target camera trajectories, quantifying rotational alignment error. | \[[137](#bib.bib137)\] |
|  TransErr | $`\downarrow`$ |  Translation Error |  Euclidean distance between recovered and target camera trajectories, quantifying translational alignment error. | \[[137](#bib.bib137)\] |
| ![\[Uncaptioned image\]](2509.07996v4/figures/icons/metrics.png) Generation Quality - Human Preference |  |  |  |  |
|  VQ | $`\uparrow`$ |  Visual Quality (2AFC) |  Win rates for perceptual visual quality in two-alternative forced choice comparisons, reflecting human-preferred realism of generations. | \[[299](#bib.bib299)\] |
|  MR | $`\uparrow`$ |  Motion Rationality (2AFC) |  Win rates for perceived motion rationality in two-alternative forced choice settings, evaluating naturalness of temporal dynamics. | \[[299](#bib.bib299)\] |
|  DMOS | $`\uparrow`$ |  Differential Mean Opinion Score |  Average human-rated alignment with conditioning constraints (e.g., scene graphs), providing a relative perceptual quality measure. | \[[196](#bib.bib196)\] |
| ![\[Uncaptioned image\]](2509.07996v4/figures/icons/metrics.png) Forecasting Quality - Spatial Predictive Accuracy |  |  |  |  |
|  L1 Error | $`\downarrow`$ |  Frame Mean Absolute Error |  Pixel- or depth-space L1 distance between predicted and ground-truth frames, quantifying reconstruction fidelity and short-horizon accuracy. | \[[322](#bib.bib322)\] |
|  L2 Error | $`\downarrow`$ |  Frame Mean Squared Error |  Pixel- or depth-space L2 distance between predicted and ground-truth frames, reflecting average squared reconstruction deviation. | \[[322](#bib.bib322)\] |
|  $`{\mathbf{{IoU}}}_{\mathrm{c}}`$ | $`\uparrow`$ |  IoU at Current Timestamp |  Intersection-over-Union between predicted and reference occupancy maps at the current frame, assessing immediate prediction quality. | \[[206](#bib.bib206)\] |
|  $`\mathbf{IoU}_{\mathrm{f}}`$ | $`\uparrow`$ |  IoU at Future Timestamp |  Intersection-over-Union between predicted and reference occupancy maps at a fixed future horizon, capturing long-range prediction quality. | \[[206](#bib.bib206)\] |
|  $`{\mathbf{IoU}}_{\mathrm{wf}}`$ | $`\uparrow`$ |  IoU at Weighted Future Timestamp |  Weighted average Intersection-over-Union across multiple future frames, emphasizing near-term predictions for smoother accuracy assessment. | \[[206](#bib.bib206)\] |
|  CD | $`\downarrow`$ |  Chamfer Distance |  Bidirectional nearest-neighbor distance between point clouds from ray-cast predicted and ground-truth occupancy, measuring geometric fidelity. | \[[381](#bib.bib381)\] |
|  L1 Med | $`\downarrow`$ |  Median L1 Depth Error |  Median absolute depth error along LiDAR rays after projection, robustly quantifying accuracy against outliers. | \[[381](#bib.bib381)\] |
|  AbsRel Med | $`\downarrow`$ |  Median Absolute Relative Error |  Median of relative depth errors across all LiDAR rays, providing a scale-aware and robust measure of accuracy. | \[[381](#bib.bib381)\] |
|  L1 Mean | $`\downarrow`$ |  Mean L1 Depth Error |  Mean absolute depth error along projected LiDAR rays, reflecting average deviation in meters from reference. | \[[381](#bib.bib381)\] |
|  AbsRel Mean | $`\downarrow`$ |  Mean Absolute Relative Error |  Mean of relative depth errors across all rays, capturing overall scale-consistent accuracy of depth predictions. | \[[381](#bib.bib381)\] |
| ![\[Uncaptioned image\]](2509.07996v4/figures/icons/metrics.png) Forecasting Quality - Temporal Predictive Accuracy |  |  |  |  |
|  KODP | $`\uparrow`$ |  Key Object Dimension Probability |  Probability-based measure penalizing implausible object dimensions using category priors, encouraging physically realistic and consistent generation. | \[[303](#bib.bib303)\] |
|  TFSC | $`\uparrow`$ |  Temporal Foreground Shape Consistency |  Voxel-level Intersection-over-Union of dynamic object instances across consecutive frames, ensuring shape persistence and temporal stability. | \[[303](#bib.bib303)\] |
|  TBEC | $`\uparrow`$ |  Temporal Background Environment Consistency |  Consistency of static voxels under ego-motion compensation, validating environmental rigidity and long-term background stability. | \[[303](#bib.bib303)\] |
| ![\[Uncaptioned image\]](2509.07996v4/figures/icons/metrics.png) Planning Quality - Open-Loop Planning |  |  |  |  |
|  ADE | $`\downarrow`$ |  Average Displacement Error |  Mean displacement error between predicted trajectories and expert waypoints across the horizon, reflecting overall trajectory accuracy. | \[[351](#bib.bib351)\] |
|  FDE | $`\downarrow`$ |  Final Displacement Error |  Displacement error at the final predicted waypoint compared with expert trajectories, emphasizing long-term accuracy. | \[[351](#bib.bib351)\] |
|  SLE | $`\downarrow`$ |  Speed L1 Error |  Mean absolute error of predicted speed control signals. | \[[129](#bib.bib129)\] |
|  SALE | $`\downarrow`$ |  Steer Angle L1 Error |  Mean absolute error of predicted steering angle control signals. | \[[129](#bib.bib129)\] |
|  CR | $`\downarrow`$ |  Collision rate |  Fraction of rollouts in which the controlled vehicle collides with surrounding agents or obstacles, indicating safety risk. | \[[304](#bib.bib304)\] |
|  PDMS | $`\uparrow`$ |  Predictive Driver Model Score |  Aggregate score combining progress, spacing, and comfort after discarding unsafe rollouts, approximating human-like driving quality. | \[[52](#bib.bib52)\] |
|  EPDMS | $`\uparrow`$ |  Extended Predictive Driver Model Score |  Extended version of PDMS that includes nine additional factors to reflect rule adherence and recovery behaviors. | \[[33](#bib.bib33)\] |
|  AHE | $`\downarrow`$ |  Average Heading Error |  Mean absolute angular deviation between predicted and expert heading over the trajectory horizon, measuring orientation accuracy. | \[[29](#bib.bib29)\] |
|  FHE | $`\downarrow`$ |  Final Heading Error |  Absolute angular deviation of predicted heading from expert at the final timestep, reflecting terminal orientation accuracy. | \[[29](#bib.bib29)\] |
|  MR | $`\downarrow`$ |  Miss Rate |  Fraction of prediction timesteps where displacement error exceeds horizon-specific thresholds, reflecting failure in trajectory coverage. | \[[29](#bib.bib29)\] |
| ![\[Uncaptioned image\]](2509.07996v4/figures/icons/metrics.png) Planning Quality - Closed-Loop Planning |  |  |  |  |
|  SR | $`\uparrow`$ |  Success Rate |  Percentage of navigation episodes that successfully reach the goal within a fixed time budget, indicating overall task completion. | \[[64](#bib.bib64)\] |
|  ID | $`\uparrow`$ |  Infraction Distance |  Average driving distance between two infractions, with longer distances reflecting safer and more reliable policy behavior. | \[[64](#bib.bib64)\] |
|  ADS | $`\uparrow`$ |  Arena Driving Score |  Composite score combining route completion metrics with PDMS to summarize closed-loop driving performance in Arena environments. | \[[356](#bib.bib356)\] |
|  NAC | $`\uparrow`$ |  No At-Fault Collisions |  Fraction of scenarios without ego-fault collisions, focusing exclusively on responsibility-aware collision evaluation. | \[[29](#bib.bib29)\] |
|  DAC | $`\uparrow`$ |  Drivable-Area Compliance |  Boolean evaluation that checks whether the ego vehicle remains inside drivable polygons throughout the rollout. | \[[29](#bib.bib29)\] |
|  DDC | $`\uparrow`$ |  Driving-Direction Compliance |  Boolean evaluation verifying that ego motion remains aligned with the designated lane’s legal driving direction. | \[[29](#bib.bib29)\] |
|  MP | $`\uparrow`$ |  Making Progress |  Boolean check confirming that the ego vehicle makes sufficient forward route progress within the evaluation horizon. | \[[29](#bib.bib29)\] |
|  TTC | $`\uparrow`$ |  Time-to-Collision |  Boolean verification that the time-to-collision value exceeds safety thresholds, preventing imminent crashes. | \[[29](#bib.bib29)\] |
|  PAR | $`\uparrow`$ |  Progress Along Route |  Ratio of ego-vehicle progress compared to expert trajectory progress along the same route, reflecting efficiency. | \[[29](#bib.bib29)\] |
|  SLC | $`\uparrow`$ |  Speed-Limit Compliance |  Score penalizing magnitude and duration of speed-limit violations, higher values indicating safer speed adherence. | \[[29](#bib.bib29)\] |
|  Comfort | $`\uparrow`$ |  Driving Comfort |  Penalization of excessive jerk, acceleration, or yaw-rate, reflecting ride quality and passenger comfort. | \[[29](#bib.bib29)\] |
| ![\[Uncaptioned image\]](2509.07996v4/figures/icons/metrics.png) Reconstruction Quality - Photometric Fidelity |  |  |  |  |
|  PSNR | $`\uparrow`$ |  Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio |  Logarithmic ratio of maximum possible signal power to reconstruction error, measuring pixel-level fidelity of generated images | \[[123](#bib.bib123)\] |
|  SSIM | $`\uparrow`$ |  Structural Similarity Index Measure |  Quality index considering structural similarity, luminance consistency, and contrast preservation between generated and reference images. | \[[306](#bib.bib306)\] |
|  LPIPS | $`\downarrow`$ |  Learned Perceptual Image Patch Similarity |  Feature-space distance between deep network activations of image patches, quantifying perceptual realism beyond pixel fidelity. | \[[382](#bib.bib382)\] |
| ![\[Uncaptioned image\]](2509.07996v4/figures/icons/metrics.png) Reconstruction Quality - View Changing Consistency |  |  |  |  |
|  NTA-IoU | $`\uparrow`$ |  Novel Trajectory Agent IoU |  Intersection-over-Union between projected 3D bounding boxes of foreground agents and detected 2D boxes under novel viewpoints. | \[[390](#bib.bib390)\] |
|  NTL-IoU | $`\uparrow`$ |  Novel Trajectory Lane IoU |  Intersection-over-Union between projected lane structures and detected lane markings from novel viewpoints, evaluating background alignment. | \[[390](#bib.bib390)\] |
| ![\[Uncaptioned image\]](2509.07996v4/figures/icons/metrics.png) Downstream Evaluation - Detection |  |  |  |  |
|  mAP | $`\uparrow`$ |  Mean Average Precision |  Average precision computed over multiple IoU thresholds for 2D detection boxes on standard benchmarks, reflecting detection accuracy. | \[[180](#bib.bib180)\] |
|  mAP-3D | $`\uparrow`$ |  Mean Average Precision in 3D |  Average precision for 3D bounding boxes, integrating precision-recall across multiple IoU thresholds in 3D space. | \[[28](#bib.bib28)\] |
|  LET-3D-AP | $`\uparrow`$ |  Average Precision with Longitudinal Error Tolerance |  3D average precision using LET-IoU, allowing depth shifts along the camera ray within a tolerance margin. | \[[122](#bib.bib122)\] |
|  LET-3D-APL | $`\uparrow`$ |  Longitudinal Affinity Weighted LET-3D-AP |  Weighted LET-3D-AP that penalizes larger longitudinal corrections, improving realism in depth-sensitive evaluation. | \[[122](#bib.bib122)\] |
|  mATE | $`\downarrow`$ |  Mean Average Translation Error |  Mean L2 distance between predicted and ground-truth object centers for matched true positives, evaluating localization accuracy. | \[[28](#bib.bib28)\] |
|  mASE | $`\downarrow`$ |  Mean Average Scale Error |  Mean scale discrepancy defined as one minus IoU for matched true positives, reflecting object size accuracy. | \[[28](#bib.bib28)\] |
|  mAOE | $`\downarrow`$ |  Mean Average Orientation Error |  Mean absolute yaw error of matched true positives, quantifying accuracy of predicted object orientation. | \[[28](#bib.bib28)\] |
|  mAVE | $`\downarrow`$ |  Mean Average Velocity Error |  Mean L2 difference between predicted and ground-truth object velocities for matched true positives, measuring motion accuracy. | \[[28](#bib.bib28)\] |
|  mAAE | $`\downarrow`$ |  Mean Average Attribute Error |  Mean error in predicting semantic attributes for matched true positives, computed as one minus attribute accuracy. | \[[28](#bib.bib28)\] |
|  NDS | $`\uparrow`$ |  nuScenes Detection Score |  Composite metric combining mAP with normalized true positive errors (mATE, mASE, mAOE, mAVE, mAAE), reflecting holistic detection quality. | \[[28](#bib.bib28)\] |
| ![\[Uncaptioned image\]](2509.07996v4/figures/icons/metrics.png) Downstream Evaluation - Segmentation |  |  |  |  |
|  mIoU | $`\uparrow`$ |  Mean Intersection over Union |  Average Intersection-over-Union across all semantic classes in 2D images or 3D point clouds, measuring segmentation accuracy. | \[[68](#bib.bib68)\] |
|  BEV-Map-IoU | $`\uparrow`$ |  Bird’s-Eye-View Map IoU |  Class-wise Intersection-over-Union for freespace, lane, and dynamic agents in BEV compared with HD maps, evaluating scene consistency. | \[[243](#bib.bib243)\] |
| ![\[Uncaptioned image\]](2509.07996v4/figures/icons/metrics.png) Downstream Evaluation - Tracking |  |  |  |  |
|  MOTA | $`\uparrow`$ |  Multi-Object Tracking Accuracy |  Composite metric aggregating false positives, false negatives, and identity switches into a single score, evaluating overall tracking reliability. | \[[18](#bib.bib18)\] |
|  MOTP | $`\uparrow`$ |  Multi-Object Tracking Precision |  Mean Intersection-over-Union between matched predictions and ground truth across frames, quantifying spatial localization precision. | \[[18](#bib.bib18)\] |
|  3D-AMOTA | $`\uparrow`$ |  Average Multi-Object Tracking Accuracy in 3D |  3D extension of MOTA averaged across recall thresholds, reducing sensitivity to threshold choices while evaluating tracking accuracy. | \[[313](#bib.bib313)\] |
|  3D-AMOTP | $`\uparrow`$ |  Average Multi-Object Tracking Precision in 3D |  3D extension of MOTP averaging localization precision (IoU or center distance) over recall thresholds, reflecting robustness. | \[[313](#bib.bib313)\] |
| ![\[Uncaptioned image\]](2509.07996v4/figures/icons/metrics.png) Downstream Evaluation - Occupancy Prediction |  |  |  |  |
|  Occupancy-IoU | $`\uparrow`$ |  Occupancy Intersection over Union |  Intersection-over-Union between predicted and labeled voxel occupancies at class- or scene-level granularity, reflecting occupancy accuracy. | \[[269](#bib.bib269)\] |
|  VPQ | $`\uparrow`$ |  Voxelized Panoptic Quality |  Panoptic quality metric for voxelized outputs, combining semantic segmentation accuracy with instance detection recall into a unified score. | \[[206](#bib.bib206)\] |
| ![\[Uncaptioned image\]](2509.07996v4/figures/icons/metrics.png) Downstream Evaluation - VQA |  |  |  |  |
|  Top-1 Acc | $`\uparrow`$ |  Visual Question Answering Top-1 Accuracy |  Exact-match accuracy of predicted answers across diverse question categories in autonomous driving VQA tasks, measuring reasoning reliability. | \[[309](#bib.bib309)\] |

Table 14: Summary of the evaluation metrics used for evaluating the
quality of ¹Generation, ²Forecasting, ³Planning, ⁴Reconstruction, and
⁵Downstream Tasks for the VideoGen, OccGen, and LiDARGen models in 2D,
3D, and 4D tasks.
